## Purpose

Define every material logical relationship and constraint, its enforcement layer, historical behavior, and tenant-integrity consequence.

## Scope

This catalog covers 469 relationships and 785 constraints. It describes future DDL intent but is not executable SQL. MySQL foreign keys are immediate, not deferrable; parent creation order is mandatory. User-partitioned InnoDB tables cannot participate in ordinary foreign keys, so official relational tables are not partitioned initially.

## Foreign-key behavior

| Behavior | Approved boundary |
| --- | --- |
| RESTRICT | Default for authoritative parents, official records, identity, Consent, provenance and governed references. |
| CASCADE | Allowed only for inseparable non-authoritative join children after explicit migration review; none is assumed in this baseline. |
| SET NULL | Allowed only when absence is a valid historical fact and does not hide authority; no mandatory relationship uses it. |
| Application-managed archival | Marks lifecycle state and moves or protects evidence without breaking identifiers. |
| Supersession | Links former and successor official records; both remain verifiable. |
| Anonymization | Removes or transforms approved personal fields while retaining integrity/provenance under a recorded decision and hold check. |

Cascades shall not erase Score Sheets, Results, Appeals, Certificates, Consent history, Quran releases, provenance, audit evidence, or protected media evidence.

## Required constraint-class realization

| Constraint class | Realization |
| --- | --- |
| Workspace ownership | Non-null `workspace_id`, Workspace FK, and `UNIQUE(workspace_id, id)` on every TENANT_OWNED/TENANT_CHILD table. |
| Cross-Workspace prevention | Composite child `(workspace_id, parent_id)` to parent `(workspace_id, id)` whenever both records are tenant-scoped. |
| Unique active contact | Generated/status-aware nullable active lookup hash; MySQL permits multiple NULL historical rows but one active hash. |
| Unique active public handle | Nullable generated `active_handle_key` with unique constraint under OD-053; handle authority never exceeds profile policy. |
| Membership/assignment/panel/registration | Purpose-specific unique constraints preserve one active lineage in the declared Workspace/resource scope. |
| Active Guardian relationship | Status-aware generated relationship key plus subject/guardian/scope validation and transaction. |
| Participant Snapshot | Unique Registration/version, immutable checksum/version and repository prohibition on update. |
| Score Sheet and items | Unique Judge/Performance lineage, version and criterion uniqueness, exact non-negative DECIMAL checks and Ruleset validation. |
| Results and Certificates | Result version/serial/code uniqueness, immutable former versions, explicit acyclic supersession and RESTRICT deletion. |
| Social relationships | Follow/reaction/bookmark/block/mute actor-target/resource uniqueness plus status-aware lifecycle where multiple historical rows are retained. |
| Idempotency and Outbox | Scoped key/event identity uniqueness in the same authoritative transaction. |
| Taxonomy/Quran reference | Governed code uniqueness, release checksum and Release/Reading/Surah/Ayah identity uniqueness; exact canonical text has binary collation. |
| Time/version/sequence | Same-row checks plus Core PHP transaction rules for cross-row monotonicity and cycle prevention. |
| Self-approval | Authorization/application policy plus separate Approval Decision records; MySQL cannot prove actor-category independence alone. |

## Business-invariant enforcement layers

| Invariant | MySQL and application enforcement |
| --- | --- |
| INV-001 | workspace_id NOT NULL; Workspace FK; candidate key; tenant-context policy; authorization and negative tests |
| INV-002 | Composite Workspace-aware FK; repository policy; transaction; cross-tenant attack test |
| INV-003 | Separate persons and user_accounts tables/aggregates; architecture and migration tests |
| INV-004 | Role/Membership/Assignment relations; conflict policy; authorization tests |
| INV-005 | competition_editions -> competition_series FK; non-null relationship |
| INV-006 | Separate organizer/scope/geography/eligibility/representation relations |
| INV-007 | participant_snapshots immutable version/checksum; no update repository; correction workflow |
| INV-008 | score_sheet_versions append-only; unique lineage/version; optimistic lock; reopening workflow |
| INV-009 | DECIMAL(P_SCORE,S_SCORE) only; schema scan; value-object/property tests |
| INV-010 | Server calculation service; client payload rejection; calculation trace and audit |
| INV-011 | Required performances.ruleset_version_id and immutable Ruleset Version |
| INV-012 | ruleset_versions checksum/status; approval; new-version-only rule |
| INV-013 | score_reopening_requests/approvals; Step-Up/resource policy; audit |
| INV-014 | result_snapshots plus corrections/supersession; no in-place final Result update |
| INV-015 | certificate_revocations and supersession; RESTRICT delete; verification status |
| INV-016 | Separate appeals aggregate with immutable source references and remedy events |
| INV-017 | Quran release capability; immutable release; authorization/checksum gates |
| INV-018 | Release/version/checksum uniqueness; approval and activation transaction |
| INV-019 | Object keys/hashes only in MySQL; private object policy |
| INV-020 | Guardian/Consent/media link and publication transaction |
| INV-021 | Separate social tables/module; no official-score write interface |
| INV-022 | Projection lifecycle/source_version; rebuild and stale-state tests |
| INV-023 | Outbox/idempotency uniqueness; consumer transaction and replay tests |
| INV-024 | Break-Glass scope/expiry/approval; Step-Up and immutable audit |
| INV-025 | Support case/field/time scope; per-access audit |
| INV-026 | RESTRICT DB privilege and lifecycle/hold workflow |
| INV-027 | Resource policy after lookup; public ID provides no permission |
| INV-028 | API scopes exclude score writes; owning-module interface |
| INV-029 | Hash/reference only; KMS/secrets outside schema |
| INV-030 | Workload/queue isolation; bounded jobs and load tests |

MySQL is credited only for declared key, foreign-key, unique, generated-column and same-row check behavior. Cross-row policy, approval independence, monotonicity, graph acyclicity, dynamic Ruleset bounds and authorization require Core PHP domain/application services, locks, reconciliation, audit and automated tests.

## P3-B02 integrity addendum

The People migrations enforce unique active Account SELF links, unique active name-type rows, active Person-role rows,
and unique active guardian/dependent pairs using generated active markers and foreign keys. Geography associations are
validated against the B01 parent/child hierarchy. Transactional application checks additionally require the exact
self-link or active guardianship, an adult Guardian with active Guardian role, a minor dependent, and preservation of
at least one active guardian for a minor. Those cross-row rules are covered by MySQL integration and concurrency tests.
